          function getTidInfo(channel, tid) {
            if (!channel || !tid) return null
            var len = channel.length
            for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
              if (!channel[i] || !channel[i].sub) continue
              var sLen = channel[i].sub.length
              for (var j = 0; j < sLen; j++) {
                if (tid === channel[i].sub[j].tid) {

                    name: channel[i]['name'],
